[00:30:36] that AI has changed and really transformed the way that people and companies do work,
[00:30:41] I think we've been through a few different phases.

[00:31:03] You iterate on a slide deck together, those types of workflows.
[00:31:08] But I think we're really on the cusp of this new moment in AI,
[00:31:12] where AI starts to act more like a teammate or more like a colleague inside of a company.
[00:31:18] And that's really different, I think, from what's come before.

[00:31:52] human colleague if they're not a collection of tasks that you kind of outsource,
[00:31:56] but they own outcomes and they have responsibilities.
[00:31:59] And you trust them to kind of own a discrete part of a company and kind of do that work for you.

[00:32:50] that each have specific roles and different kind of lanes of work.

[00:32:58] You actually create a bot for a unit of work and a kind of job responsibility,
